yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷai nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmai kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunilai niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmaikaḷai ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vaittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum kaṟkum tollaiyai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tillai, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēvaiyumillai. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmaikaḷai ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉai aṭaiyalām.
- maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nākai - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭaittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulamai mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamaiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam uṭaiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟainta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivait tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittokai, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭaimaiyē eṉṉai ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ tokai curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷai varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭaiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭai	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷai
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟai maṟaintatē
aṭikaḷār vāḻntatu mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭē yāyiṉum, avarāl tamiḻnāṭu aṭainta payaṉ aḷapparitu eṉak kūṟutal mikaiyākātu. aṭikaḷār varaṉmuṟai vaḻātu mummoḻip pulamai peṟṟa mutuperum pulavar; caiva cittāntattiṉ tiṭpanuṭpaṅkaḷai naṉku payiṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarum meccum vakaiyil ‘Mystic Mina’ eṉṉum āṅkila veḷiyīṭṭiṉ mūlam eṅkum paravacceytavar. aṭikaḷār pulamaiyiṟ paḻamaiyum putumaiyum paṅku peṟṟōṅkiṉa. aṭikaḷār payiṉṟa nūlkaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟaṉa eṉpatai maṟaintaṉa oḻiya iruntaṉavāyoḷirum nūlkaḷaiyuṭaiya avarkaḷatu nūṟkaruvūlamē iṉitu viḷakkum.
aṭikaḷār nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulap celvarāy - nāvīṟuṭaiyarāy - eḻuttāṟṟalil evarum viyakkattakka eḻuttāḷarāy ilaṅkiyum, avarkaḷ avvāṟṟalkaḷai aṭimai vāḻvilīṭupaṭṭu vipaḻalukkiṟaitta vīṇirākkāmal, urimai vīṟuṭaṉ taṉimaiyiluḻaittup poruḷ pukaḻum īṭṭi, vaiyattu vāḻvāṅku vāḻnta vakai peritum pārāṭṭattakkatu. avvakaiyil aṭikaḷār māperuṅ kalaivīrar eṉap pōṟṟutaṟkuriyār.
uṟaṅkuvatu pōlum cākkā ṭuṟaṅki
  viḻippatu pōlum piṟappu 	(kuṟaḷ 339)
eṉṉum uṇmaiyai uḷaṅkoṇṭu, kaḻintataṟ kiraṅkātu, nammaip pirinta nallavar iṉṉuyir avartam vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuḷiṉ maṟaimalaiyaraṭikaḷiṉ maṉṉi vaṟṟā iṉpam uṟṟu viḷaṅkaveṉa maṉaṅkoḷalaṉṟi maṟṟeṉceya vallōm!
- vittuvāṉ mē.vī. vēṇukōpāla piḷḷai.
(pak. 47)

maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
nuḻaivurai
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
muntai aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum takavārnta perumai peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yuraikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟai moḻikaḷukkum tantai moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻparappāḷarāyp potunilaik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum perumai peṟṟa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭai malaiyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpatai nilai nāṭṭiya niṟaimalai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
ainteḻuttāl orupāṭai eṉṟu aṟaiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭaiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalaimali pēḻaiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟaimai pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpatai,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunilai tirital ceyyuṭ killai 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇai ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citaintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumaiyum kuṟumaiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷai ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takainiṉṟa taṇṭamiḻai - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilaikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟaimalai yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talaippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivai
 paintamiḻ kākkum paṭai
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭaiyum kalait tiṟap paṭaippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭaiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟai āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟai āṟṟalai 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalaic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattai
paṉimalai mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amaiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amaintavar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimalai - imayam; nīla āṟu - nail; amaitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷai, nūlaka iyakkat tantai araṅka nātarum, innāḷ caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakaimaippaṭutti vaittamai perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avai,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟaiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭurai 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nākai aṭikaḷai - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattaic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakaiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammaiyār! ivviṇaiyar koṭaiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉai ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷattai vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟaivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvarai payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum nilaiyil, arumait tantaiyār iyaṟkai eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉaiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyait toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmai tamiḻukku illai. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppillai eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟaikaḷaiyum iḷamaiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟaiyaiyum, nākaiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟaiyaiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟaiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟaiyāl vāṭum aṉṉaikkuc cumaiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākaiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamaiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉai māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpataiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyai niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, tokai nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvai, iṟaiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvai mukappil, ematu patiṉaintām āṇṭil muṟaippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷaiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyataiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uraiyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamaip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nākai intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc caiva cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉai maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉaipeyaril maṟuppurai eḻutiṉār. ataṉaikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷaic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉai viraivilē ceṉṉaikku varuvippōm. nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉai vāḻvu amaiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇaivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉai māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumai kaṇṭu, pērāciriyaraik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uraiyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattaiyum mūtaṟivaiyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanilai ovvāmaiyāl nākaikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉai: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaraic ceṉṉaikku aḻaittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpikai eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉai kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟait talaivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kalaiñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkalai māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullaippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappālai (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci urai eḻutiṉār.
avvurai nūlai vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟaiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvuraip pāṅkiṉaip piṉpaṟṟip paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷai ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟai urai, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakaikaḷai iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉai viḷaittamai kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakaiya uraikaḷaiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻaintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭattai viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉaik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikkai kuṟaiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟaiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyai muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikaimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. vaiyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikaimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭaivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉai kacaṭukaḷai nīkkiyatu? vaiyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammaic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟaic ceyta arumai eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal kārikai āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārait taḻuvutal uṇmai tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapurattai vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattaiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattaiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmi kaḷ valiyuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkaikaḷaiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷai oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. innilaiyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkaikaḷaiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉaikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉaiyār ciṉṉammaiyār, tuṇaiviyār cavuntiram ammai yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvaraik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpikai, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. innilaiyilēyē 27.08.1911il caivat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭai (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. muṉṉēyē avarai aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvai
nām mīḷpārvai oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akavaiyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kaivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullaippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvurai vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉai; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉai; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉai - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikaiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭamai eṉpatu, akattē amaintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷai viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyām. av veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravillai! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka nilaiyam, potunilaik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amaippu uruvākum nilaiyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avai iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa nilaiyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭait tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭait tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭait tūṇṭal valimai mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyai veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakaittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takaimai ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉai uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilaināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amaintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmai,
 tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟai yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantaip pēṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉaippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟokai, arumaṇik kuvaiyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷaik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumaimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumait tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 varai mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavai. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyavaiyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vaitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉamai, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷai mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. annilaiyil,
 ūḻaiyum uppakkam kāṇpar ulaiviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyai vāymai moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷaiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amaintu tāṅkal, iyaṟkai iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟai muṉvaittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icaik kalaikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākarattai veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉai vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉaip pakutikaḷaiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, uraivēntar auvai cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷai vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantaiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷaiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷaiyum muḻumai muḻumaiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - tokai - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷaiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti varai veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷai ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amaikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumai iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇaiyaiyum makkaḷaiyum uṟavaiyum aṉpaiyum naṇpaiyum oruṅkē iṇaittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇaippaiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
maṟaimalaiyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumai! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalaic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭait tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putaiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉaiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭai! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakaimai! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟaiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymai viḷakkamām!
 puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar nilaiyam,						iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							        irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturai - 6
625006.

malaikka vaikkum maṟaimalaiyam!
patippurai
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉai vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmai uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷatai varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇarai aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattait tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭai eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭai niṉaivu kūrum vakaiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyai eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭaippukaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimaiyūṭṭiṉar.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻai 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭaippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavai, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavai, peyar terintum pārvaikkuk kiṭṭātavai - eṉap palavakaiyāyk kiṭaittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭaippukaḷ! avaṟṟai `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum talaippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭaivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavalaiyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt ticai nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭaipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭaiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputaiyal palar kaikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷārait tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyattai etirppatilum camaṟkirutattai vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyait tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vaittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavaittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻaippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimaiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukaiyaiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kalaiñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitaikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvai avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta virutai civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamaippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malaimalaiyaṭikaḷ).
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyaraik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟaimiku āṅkilam maṟaivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulamai mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻaikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumaiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
 vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
  maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ maṟaiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉaip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉai maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkaiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻaippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamillai.
ceṉṉaiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷait tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talaimuṟaikkum varuṅkālat talaimuṟaikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉai (cātaṉai)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumaiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avai kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpatai aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalattai otukki vaittu, tamiḻ nalattaik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟaivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalaip piṉṉiṇaippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻaikkum uṇarvai iḷamaiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmai eḻavaittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avarai eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamaiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟaivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavai! avaṟṟil orupakutiyai yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kaikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vai. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvattai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉaivar kaikaḷaiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumai - caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟai aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟai aṉaivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭaikkum paṇiyaic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumaiyaik karutātu uḻaikkum maṉavurattai muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷai iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭaiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
vallamai cērkkum valimaiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ naintiṭil etunammaik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻaik kākkum uṇarvaip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kaikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷai nāṭṭuṭaimaiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.

1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉaippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷaippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟaiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amaippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimaik kaḻakam eṉṉum amaippaiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattaiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻaittavar. iḷam talaimuṟaikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkaiyai neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amaippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻaittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakattai niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyum, putiya paṭaippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum, 19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkat tilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷait tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumaiyākac cērttu poruḷvaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil orē vīccil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttirai patittu varupavar.

Preface 
The first part of the following treatise was read as Presidential Address at the All South India Saiva Con­ference held at Cuddalore, New Town, on the 16th, 17th, 18th, 19th, 20th and 21st 1929. The object of the Con­ference was to revive the study and practice of the oldest principles of the Saiva religion, which, in recent years, have greatly fallen into disuse and disrepute by being mixed up with the Puranic myths and which the modern Self­Respect movement calumniated in the most opprobrious language, without being able to discriminate between the cardinal points of Saivism and the merely adventitious ones. 
In the conflict of thoughts and ethical sentiments that arose between the Tamilians of the south and the Aryans of the north who came and settled all over the Tamil country from the first century A. D., down to the fifteenth and still later, the religion of the land, that is Saivism. underwent a marked change its outer rim, although in its centre it remained as pure as crystal and as impenetrable as a hard diamond. What is bound and true to its core, what is perfect and complete in itself, requires no change, re­quires no improvement. At the very dawn of Tamilian civilisation, of which we catch a glimpse through the Thol­kappiam the oldest Tamil work extant, we find the views about God, soul, and matter and salvation to have become thoroughly sound and complete. As our knowledge of these facts deepens, as our ideas about their relations expand, we find the conclusions reached by our forefathers about their nature and relations, daily receiving corroborations not only from the modern biological, geological, and other  scienitific discoveries, but also from the philosophical view worked out and held by such eminent thinkers as William James, Bergson, Lodge, Ward and others. 
While preserving the intrinsic nature and excellence of their religious system, the teachers and prophets of the Tamil country have not disdained to take from foreign cults. whatever seemed to have influenced the ordinary people, and whatever might be turned to good account in the light of their own religious thought. In this wise came to by added to the outer surface of the body of Saivism, simple as poetic embellishments remember, only a few Puranic­myths of the northeners at first, but, as time went on, rushed in quite a multitude of them which, instead of adding to the naked beauty of the resplendent form, concealed it and even rendered it ugly in the eyes of- an incautious, indolent, or an ill-intentioned looker-on. Only to the penetrating mental vision of a careful thinker, the intrinsic value of the system came to reveal itself. Others touched only its outer crust of myths, some of them believing them to be actual facts, while some others who had an inkling of reasoning. spirit, turned away from them with disgust. However, one who would like to be benefited by the inestimable knowledge treasured up in the Saiva system of thought, should not render himself liable to, be thus scared away by the Aryan myths that sit at its door. Dr. G. U. Pope who did an invaluable Service to: the world by translating into English the sacred Tamil poems called the Thiruva­chakam of S1, Manickavachakar wisely observes: “paura­nic mythology and legend is dragged in, as simple poetic embellishment designed to please the multitude”* 
But the religious and philosophical ideas that are developed here in the following pages have nothing to do with such Puranic myths, for they are taken entirely from the ancient Tamil work the Tholkappiam, the only treatise extant that was composed at a time when the Aryan in­fluence did not touch even the fringe of the Tamilian thought, when the creation of Puranic myths did not take place as yet. The age of this unique Tamil work goes back to 3500 B. C. as is shown in my work on S1, Manicka­vachakar. The Aryans were then spreading over the north mid founding colonies in the midst of the Tamils and Kolarians that existed there long before their advent. 
The Tholkappiam comprises both Tamil grammar and poetics and the latter classifies the ideal activities of the ancient day Tamilians under the three categories, Love Wealh, and Virtue. What to choose from their life for ideal representations in poetry and how to idealize them for which purpose, from the subjects of many a chapter in the third book on poetics. The life of the Tamils thus dwelt on exhibits its gradual emergence from the life of the most primitive kind into that of strikingly civilized one. Still a strong tinge of the primitive characteristics, even in the life of a highly civilized kind, cannot be ex­plained except on the supposition that the Tamils are a people naturally very conservative in their habits and cus­toms and that a large portion of them were then closely adhering to them even while they were adopting the ele­ments of civilisation introduced by the best among them. 
Of the many interresting subjects treated of, Love and War form the two themes that hold as prominent a place in it, as ,they had held in almost all famous literature of the world. For man was born in love, was bred in love lives in love, and passes at last into love. Care has been, therefore, taken by the author to distinguish this love from lust which is intense selfishness, which takes no thought of others and their happiness, and which is wrongly identified with the former by persons who know no more than what they sensuously feel and enjoy. The union of spirits and not the union of mere corporal matter was regarded as the result of love, by 8t. Tholkappiar and by a host of poets, salOts and sages who followed in his footsteps. Love regenerates both mind and body; it springs from the depth of a pure mind, flows out through the body and enters again into another mind by means of another body and blends the two minds into one. This mortal body serves only as an indispensable instrument for bringing about this intimate union of two pure spirits. When such a superior kind of love springs up from a mind and flows over sweeping before its irresistible and dignified march, all the inferior and harmful kinds of desires and passions, it goes not towards one mind alane, although it runs into one fit­ting centre;: for filling it up with essence, but it gaes into each and all and drenches them in its wholesome element in varying degree according to their deserts. Many a~ are the forms and degrees of love, the highest, the greatest and the sublimest manifestation of it occurs only at the junction of an young man and a woman accomplished in all ideal qualities. In its immeasurable height and depth no other form of it can bear camparision with it. The man becomes the idol of her heart, while the woman occupies his inner sanctuary, at whose altar he will never hesitate to make any sacrifice that may be required of him. Thus the two come to acquire the virtue of leading a life for the other, a virtue, as it grows, makes it possessor identify his or her interests with the interests of the righteous. 
From this form of the purest spiritual love, arises the necessity of acquiring wealth, not for filling his own wants, not for securing comforts and pleasures for his own self, but for keeping the object of his adoration, the offsprings of their love-union, the kith and kin of his heart, in un­mixed happiness and comfort. As the circle of those, whom he is thus bound by love and not by mere duty to support widens, as the number of those who lie under his protec­tion increases, ambition far wealth, power and distinction also increases, and this exercises his intelligence utmost and calls forth the play of all the innate powers of his mind. There can be no a greater incentive to stimulate the’ intellectual energies af man, and nothing can induce him to display his formidable heroic deeds more, than what offers him a stout resistance to the achievement of his objects. This consideration naturally leads the author to bring in the topic of war, since war is the only perilous kind of resistance and obstacle that a man of the highest responsi­bilities meets with in the path of his ambitious life. 
And lastly, when success has brought home to his mind, and to the mind of his partner, how full of cares, troubles, and untold miseries this earthly life is, to what great agony both the mind and body of a numberless beings human and animal are subjected by a few who seek their ease, comfort and happiness, the man and the woman, jointly direct their thoughts to the attainment of a bliss unattended by misery. Thus a fresh regeneration of their life commences in the purely spiritual atmosphere in which the aroma of divine love emanates from God, accord­ing to Thalkappiar, the ever resplendent intelligent principle, exhilarates their mind and transmutes their human love into divine. 
It is remarkable that in emphasizing the importance of turning their mutual human love to the love of God, the married couple is advised by our 81. Tholkappiar to strive after it conjointly. From this it appears that, at the time of Tholkappiar, the life of celibacy and asceticism was quite unknown. Even in the north it came into vogue only after­the rise of Buddhism and Jainism. In the pre-Buddhistic, that is, in the Vedic period, the life of the Aryan house­holder and the Aryan sage was similar to that of the Tamilian, but with this difference that, while in the former mere carnal pleasures reign supreme, in the latter an ideal and chaste love cast around its sweet and mellow light. 
On all these points I have expatiated to the best of my lights and have shown how the principles of life laid down 5000 years ago by 81. Tholkappiar in his immortal Tamil work Tholkappiam, are in striking and wonderful harmony with the truly physical, moral, intellectual, and religious requirements of advancing ffi:)dern civilisation. It is a great pleasure to me to acknowledge my gratitude to the whole modern Tamil world who, with but one strange exception, appreciated this my humble contribution to modern Tamil literature and spoke and wrote of it in very high terms. To the strange critic also, who is the type of the unprogressive conservative section, I express my grateful feelings for having given me an opportunity to defend my position more strongly than before, in the second and concluding parts. 
Pallavaram,	VEDACHALAM
The sacred order of Lover 
30th April, 1930.

1. paṇṭai nākarika makkaḷ
innilavulakam eṅkaṇum iruntu uyirvāḻntu varukiṉṟa palavēṟu makkaṭkūṭṭattāriṟ, paṇṭaikkālan toṭṭu nākarikam uṭaiyarāy vāḻntu varuvōr mikac cilarēyāvar. accilarāvār; ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, acīriyar, yūtar, cīṉar, pāracikar, āriyar, tamiḻar, mekcikar, perūviyar eṉpavarē yāvar. ivaruḷ ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, ācīriyar, yūtar eṉṉum aimperuṅkūṭṭattārum ivintiya nāṭṭukkup puṟampē mēṟkellaiyil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷil iruntōr āvar; cīṉar pāracikar āriyar eṉpār iv intiya nāṭṭukku vaṭakkiluḷḷa mērumalaip pakkattiliruntu teṟkum mēṟkuṅ kiḻukkuñ ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkuk kuṭipukuntu vaikiṉōrāvar; tamiḻar eṉpār vaṭakkē imayamalaiyiliruntu teṟkē kaṭalil amiḻntip pōṉa kumari nāṭṭiṉ ellai muṭiya iruntōr āvar; mekcikar perūviyar eṉpār naṭu amerikkāvilum ataṟkuc ciṟitu teṟkēyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷilum iruntōr āvar. ippalavēṟu makkaṭkūṭṭattāriṉ nākarika vāḻkkaiyellām iṟṟaikku aiyāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē naṭaipeṟṟaṉa ākum. ivartam vāḻkkaiyiṉ varalāṟukaḷai nām uḷḷapaṭi uṇaral vēṇṭumāyiṉ, ivarkaḷ paṇṭai
nāḷilē kaṭṭivaitta kuvinta kōpuraṅkaḷum, nilattiṉkīḻp putainta araṇmaṉaikaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ cuvarkaḷiluṅ kaḷimaṇ palakaikaḷilum poṟittuvaitta aracar varalāṟukaḷum, avarkaḷ iyaṟṟivaitta nūlkaḷumē ataṟkuk karuviyāyiruntu utavukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ utavi koṇṭu ippaṇṭai makkaḷiṉ nākarika varalāṟukaḷai nām ārāyntu pārkkuṅkāṟ, cīṉar pāracikar āriyar eṉṉum mūṉṟu vakuppārait tavira maṟṟaiyōriṉ nākarika vāḻkkai ēṟakkuṟaiya otta nilaiyiṉatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, acīriyar, yūtar eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭu nākarika makkaḷ vakuppiṉaril ekupatiyariṉ nākarikavāḻkkaiyē, ēṉai ellārtam nākarika vāḻkkaiyiṉum mikka paḻaimaiyuñ ciṟappum uṭaiyatāka nuvalap paṭukiṉṟatu. iṟṟaikku eṇṇāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē iv ekupatiyar araciyaṟ ṟuṟaiyilum, oppuravu vāḻkkaiyilum, camaya uṇarcciyilum, nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ amaippatilun, tirukkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭuvatilum, ēṉai nākarika makkaḷellāruṅ kaṇṭu tikaikkattakka attaṉai tiṟamaiyum aṟivum celvamum paṭaittiruntaṉar eṉa avartam nākarika varalāṟṟiṉai naṉku ārāyntuṇarnta or āciriyar pirakcu pē (Brugsch Bey) pakarkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam eṇṇāyira āṇṭu kaṭku muṉṉamē nākarika vāḻkkaiyil talaiciṟantu niṉṟavarākac collappaṭutaṟku ēṟṟa meyccāṉṟukaḷ vāytta paṇṭai nākarika makkaḷ ekupatiyarait tavira vēṟevarumillai yeṉpatē varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyarkaḷiṉ muṭipāyiṉum, ekupati nāṭṭil teṟalpaḵṟi (Der - el Bahri) eṉṉum ūriṉkaṇ uḷḷa aṣtāp (Hashto) araciyiṉ kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭirukkum ōviya eḻuttuk kalveṭṭukkaḷil ivekupatiyar teṉkaṭalakattuk kumarināṭṭilirunta pāṇṭi (Punt) tēyattiliruntu ceṉṟu nīlayāṟṟaṅkaraikkaṭ kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpatu viḷakkamākac collap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.1 ic ciṟanta paḻaṅ kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟu koṇṭum, iṅṅaṉamē, cālaṭi nāṭṭiṉkaṇ akaḻntu kaṇṭa cālapperiya araṇmaṉaikaḷ kōyilkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta kaḷimaṇ palakaikaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa varalāṟu kaḷāṟ pulaṉākun tamiḻppeyarkaḷ koṇṭum, avaṟṟōṭu uṭaṉakaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭut tēkkumarat tuṇṭaṅkaḷ iṟṟaikku aiyāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cālaṭiyaratu ūr eṉṉun talainakariṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa avvaraṇmaṉaikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭamai koṇṭum,2 iṟṟaikku mūvāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa viviliyanūṟ pakutiyil mayiṟṟōkai, akil mutalāṉa centamiḻc coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭutal koṇṭum3 āriya moḻi irukkuvētattum paḻantamiḻmoḻit tol kāppiyattuṅ kāṇappaṭuṅ kuṟippukaḷ koṇṭum nam paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ iṟṟaikkup pattāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarēyē nākarika vāḻkkaiyil talaiciṟantu niṉṟār
kaḷeṉpatum, attamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟic cālaṭi, ekupati mutalāṉa mēṉāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkut tamatu nākarika vāḻviṉai nāṭṭiya tamiḻvaḻi makkaḷē cālaṭiyarum ekupatiyarum avarkku iṉamāṉa ēṉai mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷum āvareṉpatum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, naṭu amerikkāvilun teṉ amerikkāvilum ēḻāyira āṇṭu kaṭkumuṉ uyirvāḻnta makkaḷun tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟavarkaḷē eṉpataṟku vēṇṭuñ cāṉṟukaḷ pulaṉāy varukiṉṟaṉa.4 iṅṅaṉan tamiḻnāṭṭilirunta makkaḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷākavō, allatavarōṭum avar tam vāḻkkaiyōṭun toṭarpuṭaiyarākavō kāṇappaṭum ippaṇṭai makkaḷiṉ nākarika vāḻkkaiyiṉ varalāṟukaḷ attaṉaiyuñ ciṟitēṟakkuṟaiya otta taṉmaiyavāy iruttal peritum viyakkaṟpāla toṉṟām.

2. nākarikam
nākarikamāṉatu akamum puṟamuḷḷa irutaṉmaip paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ akanākarikamāṉatu aṉpu eṉṉum nekiḻnta iyaṟkaiyiṉai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu nikaḻvatu. maṟṟup, puṟanākarikamō celvam eṉṉum vaṉporuḷiṉai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu nikaḻvatu. akattē naṭaipeṟum aṉpoḻukkam mēṉmēl uram peṟṟu vaḷartaṟkup, puṟattēyuḷḷa celvam utaviceyyum muṟaiyil oḻukum makkaḷiṭattē tāṉ, akanākarikamum puṟanākarikamum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu māṟukoḷḷātāy oruṅkottu, ammakkaḷukku immai maṟumai iṉpaṅkaḷai nirampa ūṭṭi, avar tammai maṅkānilaiyiṟ poṅka vaikkum. avvāṟu akavoḻukkamāṉa aṉpu vaḷarccikkut tuṇaipuriyāmal, uyiriṉaip puṉitappaṭuttum aṉpu ceṉṟavaḻiyē tāṉ cellāmal, ellā nilaikaḷiṉum mēmpaṭṭa nilaikkaṇ vaikum aṉpukkut tāṉ aṭaṅki naṭavāmal, tāṉ perukutaṟku aṉpiṉai oru tuṇaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttit, tāṉ muṉcelluñ celavukku aṉpiṉaip piṉcella vaittu, aṉpu niṟṟaṟku uriya uyarnilaiyil taṉṉai ēṟṟivaittut tāṉ niṟṟaṟkuriya kīḻnilaiyil aṉpai iṟakki vaittup, puṟanākarikap poruḷākiya celvamāṉatu taṉṉai maṟantu kūttāṭumāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam atu kūttāṭutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭukkum makkaḷiṭaiya akanākarimum puṟanākarikamum oṉṟiṉoṉṟu muraṇi, avartam immai yiṉpaṅkaḷaiyum peṟa vōṭṭāmal avarkkup peruntuṉpaṅkaḷai malaipōṟ perukkic, cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil avartammai irunta iṭamun teriyāmal maṟaiyavaikkum. muṉniṟkavēṇṭiya aṉpu piṉniṟkavum, piṉniṟka vēṇṭiya celvam muṉniṟkavum muṟai māṟiṉamaiyā laṉṟō tamiḻ makkaḷō ṭotta paḻam peruñ ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, acīriyar, āriyar, mekcikar, perūviyar mutalāṉa makkaṭpiriviṉar aṭiyōṭu māyntu pōyatōṭu, avar vaḻaṅkiya moḻikaḷum, avar vakutta eḻunilaimāṭa nakaraṅkaḷum oruṅkē aḻin toḻintaṉa! avarellām avvāṟoḻiyavun, tamiḻmakkaḷ maṭṭum aṉṟu toṭṭu iṉṟukāṟum, av vuyarnilaiyiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu ciṟitu vaḻuviṉum, muṟṟum aṟṟoḻiyātu, ēṟakkuṟaiyat tamatu paṇṭaic ciṟappiṉkaṇ nilaipeṟṟa cāṉṟōr cilaraiyāyiṉum iṉṉum uṭaiyarāy uyirpiḻaittu niṟkiṉṟaṉar; avar vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻmoḻiyum ammoḻiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa arumperu nūlkaḷum ēṉaip paṇṭai moḻikaḷaip pōl, ammoḻi nūlkaḷaip pōl iṟavātu varavara uyirppēṟi ulavi varukiṉṟaṉa.

3. tolkāppiyam
ekupatiyar cālaṭiyar mutalāṉa paṇṭai makkaḷiṉ nākarika muṟaikaḷai yuṇartaṟku arumperuñ cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittāṟpōlat, tamiḻppaḻa makkaḷiṉ nākarika varalāṟukaḷai attuṇai uṟutiyāy aṟivikkum paḻaiya meyccāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittilāmaiyiṉ aṟiyāmaip pēriruḷiṟkiṭantu tuṉpuṟṟa nām, mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷkāṟum oḷi ciṟitum illāt tiṇiyiruḷiṟkiṭantu peritu varuntiya innilavukattu vaṭamuṉaikkaṇ uḷḷa makkaḷ am muḻuviruṭ kālakkaḻiviṟ cuṭarntu pēroḷi vīci vāṉiṉkaṇ tulaṅkum puttapputiya oru perumiṉṉoḷiyiṉaik kaṇṭu makiḻvatoppa, nammuṉṉōriṉ aṟivup peruveḷiyil aṟivoḷi vīcit tikaḻāniṉṟa tolkāppiyam1 eṉṉum muḻumutal nūliṉ kāṇṭakutōṟṟa viḷakkattiṉaik kaṇṭu kaḷippeytutaṟku uriyēm allēmō!  maṟṟaip paṇṭai makkaḷiṉ nākarika varalāṟukaḷai muṟṟum uṇartaṟkuk karuviyākāmal avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavē yuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāyk kiṭaitta avar taṅ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṟ poṟitta kalveṭṭukkaḷum pōṟ kuṟaipāṭuṭaiyataṉṟāy, nam aruntamiḻ makkaḷiṉ peruntiru nākarikavaralāṟu muṟṟumeṭuttu muṭiyakkūṟum muḻumatiyāy oḷirum nam tolkāppiya nūliṉ olkāp perumāṭciyiṉaik kaṇṭu kaḷikkum tavam uṭaiyēm allamō! iṟṟaikku āṟāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē, ēṉai nākarika makkaḷellām perumpālum puṟanākarikac celva mayakkiṟ kiṭantu tammaiyum uṇarāmal tām piṟavi
yeṭutta nōkkattaiyum uṇarāmal tammaiyum ulakattaiyum paṭaitta talaivaṉaiyum avaṉṟaṉ tiruvuḷakkuṟippiṉaiyum uṇarāmal vāṇāḷai vīṇāḷāyk kaḻit aññāṉṟu, appuṟanākarika miṉukkiṉai akanākarika aṉpoḻukkap pēroḷiyiṉ muṉviḷakkamaṟac ceytu, nam mutumakkaḷaip pēraṉpām iṉpap perukkiṟ paṭivittu oppuyarvillā meypperun tolkāppiya nūṟ ṟeyvamāṭciyiṉaik kāṇṭoṟuṅ kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaḷippeytutaṟkuriyam allamō! ittuṇaicciṟanta tolkāppiyat teyva mutal nūl, puṟanākarikattai akanākarikattiṉkīḻ aṭakkic, cāyam aṟṟa celvap poruḷukku aṉpiṉ eḻil tulaṅku cāyam ēṟṟi, uyiraṟṟa eṟpuṭampu pōlvatākiya celvattiṟku aṉpeṉṉum uraṅkiḷar uyir pukuttip, puṟanākarikattai akanākarikattiṉvaḻi naṭatti, nam paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ oṇṭamiḻ koḷkaiyiṉai āyntāyntu viḷakkiya arumperu vakaimaiyiṉai aṭaivē ciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām.
aṭikkuṟippu
*1.	itaṉ kāla ārāycciṉai māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum ematu virinta nūliṟ kāṇka.

4. iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum
innilavulaka vāḻkkaiyil makkaḷellārum aṭaitaṟ kuriya uṟutipporuḷkaḷai iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṉum mūṉṟāka vakuttu ammūṉṟum piriviṉṟik kalanta aṉpiṉāl ūṭuruvi niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉpatu teḷivippār,
iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku
aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇai maruṅkiṉ	(kaḷaviyal 1)
eṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār aruḷic ceytamai peritum niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpālatākum. tolkāppiyat tōṭotta paḻaimaivāynta irukku ecur cāmam mutaliya āriya nūlkaḷiṉātal, allatu ēṉaippaḻaiya makkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷiṉātal, iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṉum im mūṉṟum aṉpiṉ vaḻiyē aṭaiyappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa nuvalap paṭāmaiyiṉai uṟṟuṇarntu pārppavarkkē ammūṉṟum aṉpoṭu puṇarntu aṭaiyappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa vaṟpuṟutturaikkun tolkāppiyaṉār tam uraipporuṇ mēṉmai naṉku viḷaṅkāniṟkum. tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻivanta āciriyaṉmāril talaiciṟantavarāṉa teyvappulamait tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
 aṉpiṉ vaḻiya tuyirnilai aḵtilārkku
eṉputōl pōrtta uṭampu
eṉṟum,
 aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭaittāyiṉ ilvāḻkkaip
paṇpum payaṉum atu
eṉṟum aruḷic ceytamaiyum, avarkkuppiṉ vanta caiva
camayāciriyarum, caivanāyaṉmārum, avar tamakkup piṉ iṟṟaiñāṉṟu varai irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ īṟāka vanta caivap periyārum ellām aṉpoḻukkattiṟkē talaimai tantamaiyum oru toṭarpupaṭuttu nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, toṉṟutoṭṭu itukāṟum nam tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai yāṉatu aṉpoḻukkattiṉaiyē accāṇiyāyk koṇṭu naṭaipeṟuvateṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. itaṉaic caiva cittānta nūlākiya tirukkaḷiṟṟup paṭiyārum,
aṉpēeṉ aṉpēyeṉ ṟaṉpāl aḻutaraṟṟi
aṉpē yaṉpāka aṟivaḻiyum - aṉpaṉṟit
aṉpē tiyāṉañ civārccaṉaikaḷ ceyyumavai
cāṟṟum paḻamaṉṟē tāṉ
eṉa iṟaivaṉai nōkkic ceyyum vaḻipāṭukaḷ attaṉaiyum uṇmai yaṉpiṉ puṟa nikaḻccikaḷākac ceyaṟpāla taṉai vaṟpuṟuttāniṟkiṉṟatu.

5. iṉpanilai
iṉi, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār makkaḷaṭaivataṟkuriya uṟutip poruḷkaḷ mūṉṟil iṉpam eṉpataṉai muṉvaittuk kūṟiya nuṭpam iṉṉateṉa nām ārāyntaṟital vēṇṭum. piṟaviyeṭukkummuṉ tam viḻaivu aṟivu ceyalkaḷ muḻutum maṟaivuṇṭu tuṉpuṟṟum, aṉṉaiyiṉ vayiṟṟil karuvāyp pukuntu piṟavi yeṭukkiṉṟa kālattu aṅkē mala nīrp paikaḷāl nerukkuṇṭu akaṭṭut tīyiṉāl vetumpip palavāṟu tuṉpuṟṟum, piṉṉar aṉṉai vayiṟṟai yakaṉṟu piḷḷaiyāy piṟantu palvakai nōykaḷālun tammaic cūḻa uḷḷār ceyyuṅ koṭumaikaḷālum peritu tuṉpuṟṟum iṅṅaṉamākap piṟavikku varum muṉṉēyiruntu piṟaviyeṭuttu iḷamaip paruvattai eytuṅkāṟum makkaḷ ellārum paṭuṅ koṭuntuṉpattiṟku ōr aḷavēyillai. ivvāṟu tuṉpat tilēyē paḻakivarum ēḻaimakkaḷākiya nām iṉpattiṉ iyalpu iṉṉateṉa oru ciṟitāyiṉum paḻaki yaṟintilamāyiṟ pēriṉpappēṟṟai aṭaitaṟku nampāl viruppam uṇṭākumō? uṇṭākātaṉṟē; ennēramun tuṉpattilēyē kiṭantuḻalum makkaḷil ettaṉaipēr ivvulaka vāḻkkaiyiṉai veṟuttu aiyō tammuyiraic caṭutiyil māyttukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar! maṇṭaivali vayiṟṟuvali iṭuppuvali mutaliya koṭu nōykaḷāl ōvātu tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭippōriṟ paṟpalar tammuyirait tāmākavē māyttuk koḷḷutalai aṭuttaṭuttuc ceytit tāḷkaḷāl aṟintu tikil koḷkiṉṟaṉar allamō! nalviṉai vayattāṟ cuvaiyāṉa uṇavukaḷai yuṇṭuṅ, kaṇṇārap pala kāṭcikaḷaik kaṇṭum, iṉiya icaikaḷ ariya virivuraikaḷ kēṭṭum, arumperu nūlkaḷ kaṟṟu aṟivu teḷintum, naṟumaṇam aḷaintum, ivvāṟellām aimpulaṉkaḷiṉ vāyilāka varum palatiṟa iṉpaṅkaḷai nukarntu makiḻtaliṉāṟṟāṉ, makkaḷuyir iḷamaip paruvam eytum varaiyilātu innilavukattē nilavukiṉṟatu! nām piṟaviyeṭukkum muṉṉamē iṟaivaṉ iv aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷait tarum palvakaip paṇṭaṅkaḷai iṅku amaittu vaittilaṉāyiṉ, nām oru noṭip poḻutāyiṉum iṅkē uyirvāḻtal iyalumō? kūrntu pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! ivvaimpula nukarccipporuḷkaḷum vāyppāka naṉku amaikkappaṭṭiruttaliṉālētāṉ palakōṭik kaṇakkāṉa palvakai makkaṭ piriviṉar tam uyirvāḻkkaiyum in nilavulakattiṉkaṇ āṅkāṅku naṭaipeṟā niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṉi aimpula nukarccikaḷāl varum iṉpattaḷavil makkaḷuyir amaitipeṟuṅ kālam evvaḷavu? peṇmakkaḷāyiṉ maṅkaipparuvam eytum varaiyilum, āṇmakkaḷāyiṉ iḷamaip paruvam eytum varaiyilumō maṟṟu apparuvam vanta aḷavilō, atukāṟuṅ kāṇakkiṭaiyā vēṟoru makiḻccik kiṭamām vēṟoru pututēyattaik kaṇṭāloppap, putiyatōr iṉpa uṇarcci varappeṟṟu avviṉpattaip peṟutalil iruvaruyirum peruvēṭkaiyum perumuyaṟciyum uṭaiyavākiṉṟaṉa. atuvaraiyil nukarntuvanta aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷellām, ippōtu tōṉṟāniṉṟa ipputiya iṉpattiṉmuṉ nillāvāy, ataṉiṟ ṟāḻntavāy, ataṉaip peṟātavaḻi ataṉāl ēkkamuṟuvārkkut tuṉpan
tarum peṟṟiyavāy māṟiviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟu iḷamaip paruvattil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārkkun tōṉṟuvatākiya iṉpa vuṇarcciyāṉatu, aḻaku aṟivu nalliyaṟkaiyiṉāl untappaṭṭu eḻum aṉpiṉ vayamāki oruvarai yoruvar iṉṟiyamaiyārāy oruṅku poruntum āṇ peṇ makkaḷukku avviḻumiya kātaliṉpattiṉai ūṭṭi, avaratu vāḻkkaiyait tūymai ceytu ataṉaip peritum payaṉpaṭac ceyvatākum. ikkātaliṉpa uṇarcci tōṉṟutaṟku muṉṉellām piḷḷaimaip paruvattiluḷḷār tāntām peṟṟa aimpula iṉpap poruḷkaḷait tāntāmē nukarvatiṟ karuttuṭaiya raṉṟit, tammō ṭotta maṟṟaip piḷḷaikaṭku avaṟṟai eḷitiṟkoṭār; tām nukarntu miñciya miccan tamakku vēṇṭātiruntālmaṭṭum, ataṉai ēṉaic ciṟuvarkkuk koṭukka orōvorukāl maṉam oruppaṭuvar; eṉṟālun, tām nukarntu kaḻitta miccattaiyum piṟarkkuk koṭukka maṉam icaiyāta ciṟārē ulakiṟ perumpālār. ivvāṟākat, taṉṉalam oṉṟē karutum piḷḷaimaip
paruvan tāṇṭi, iḷamaipparuvam pukuntu, kātaliṉpa uṇarcci vayattarāy ōrāṇmakaṉum oru peṇmakaḷum oruvarpā loruvar mikka aṉpu mītūrappeṟṟu oruṅku poruntiya kālan toṭṭō, kaṇavaṉāyiṉāṉ taṉnalaṅ karututal viṭṭut taṉ maṉaiviyiṉ nalaṅ karutuvāṉāy, avaṭku vēṇṭum nukar poruḷkaḷellān tokuttuk koṭukkum poruṭṭu muyalum palvakai muyaṟcikaḷāl ulakattiṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭu kiṉṟāṉ; aṅṅaṉamē maṉaiviyāyiṉāḷun taṉṉaip pēṇuṅ karuttai viṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉaiyē taṉṉuyirākak karuti avaṉai ōmputalilun; tam aṉpiṉ cērkkaiyāṟ piṟakkum iṉpak kuḻavikaṭkut tāṉ varaintu vaittavaikaḷai yellāṅ koṭuttu avaraik kaṇṇuṅ
karuttumāy pōṟṟi vaḷarttalilum aruḷoḻukka muṭaiya aṟavōrkku vēṇṭuva koṭuttalum aṉpoḻukkamuṭaiya aṟavōrkku vēṇṭuva koṭuttalum antaṇaraik kāttalum tuṟaviṉarai ēṟṟu avarkkut toṇṭu puritalum viruntu varuvārai ōmputalumākiya aṟampuri vāḻkkaiyilun talai niṉṟu immaṇṇulaka vāḻkkaiyiṉai viṇṇulaka vāḻkkaiyeṉap puṉitamākkit tāṉum mikap payaṉ paṭukiṉṟāḷ. pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! iṅṅaṉam āṇpeṇ eṉṉum irupālāruṅ kātaliṉ uṇarcci vayattarāy oruṅku iyaintamaiyālaṉṟō, avar tāmum iṉpuṟṟup piṟaraiyum iṉpuṟuttip, piṟavikku varāmal aruvanilaiyil aṟiyāmai yāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭuk kiṭanta uyirkaḷ uruvanilaiyil tampāṟ piḷḷaikaḷāyp piṟavi yeṭuttu aṟivum iṉpamum peṟap pērutavipurintu avvāṟṟāl iṟaivaṉṟaṉ aruṭceyaṟku ōr oppaṟṟa karuviyāyp payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ikkātaliṉpa uṇarcciyum ataṟkēṟṟa amaippum makkaḷpāl illai yāyiṉ, ivvulakam eṅkē! ivvulakil naṭaipeṟum oḻukkaṅkaḷ eṅkē! yār evarkku uṟavu! yār evarkkut tuṇai! ellām veṟum pāḻāy muṭiyumaṉṟō? evvāṟṟāl ārāyntu pārppiṉum ikkātaliṉpamāṉatu uṭampukku uyirpōlvatāy, iruḷniṟainta iravil iruḷakaṟṟum muḻumati pōlvatāyp, puṟpūṇṭukaḷ mutal nilaiyiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum vilaṅku puḷ makkaḷ mutal iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum aṟivum muyaṟciyum peṟac cuṭaroḷi parappum ñāyiṟu pōlvatāy, aṟiyāmaiyāṟ ciṉattāṟ poṟāmaiyāl iṟumāppāl oruvarō ṭoruvar aḷavaḷāvutal illāta makkaḷai aḷavaḷāvap pukuttum iṟaivaṉṟaṉ aruḷviḷakkam pōlvatāy mutaṉmai peṟṟu vayaṅkutal kaṇṭaṉaṟō tavattāṉ muṟṟuṇarvu vāynta āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉa iṉpattai mutal nilaikkaṇ vattu ōtuvārāyiṉar.
immutalāciriyarkkup piṉvanta tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ, ulakiyaloḻukkat tiṟku muṟṟum māṟāṉa pautta camaṇa camayak koḷkaikaḷāṟ ciṟitu ciṟitu īrppuṇṭu,
 ciṟappuṭai marapiṟ poruḷum iṉpamum
aṟattu vaḻippaṭūun tōṟṟam pōla
eṉa aṟattiṟkē mutalnilai kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, tiruvaḷḷuva ṉārum aṟattai muṉ vaittup poruḷai ataṉ piṉ vaittu iṉpattai iṟutikkaṇ vaittu nūl ceytārāyiṉum, iṉpattai nutaliyaṉṟi aṟañceyvāraiyum poruḷ ceyvāraiyum yāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilamākaliṉ, tolkāppiya ṉār iṉpattai mutalvaitta muṟaiyē makkaḷiyaṟkaiyoṭu poruntuvatātal naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. tamatu kālappōkkiṉai yoṭṭit tiruvaruḷḷuvaṉār  aṟattai muṉvaittu iṉpattai iṟutik kaṇ vaittu nūliyaṟṟappukuntaṉarāyiṉum, iṉpattai vāyilākakkoṇṭaṉṟi aṟaṉum illaṟan tuṟavaṟam eṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, kātaliṉpattiṉ vaḻippaṭuvatākiya illaṟattaiyē mutaṟkaṇ vaittu nūl aruḷic ceytaṉar. avvāṟṟāl āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭa muṟaiyē naṉmuṟaiyeṉpatu tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārkkum uṭam pāṭātal peṟutum. iḵtu avar illaṟaviyaliṉ mutalatikārattil, 
 aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭaittāyiṉ ilvāḻkkaip 
paṇpum payaṉum atu
eṉa aṉpai muṉvaittu aṟaṉai ataṉpiṉ vaittut teḷittōtiyatu koṇṭum iṉitu peṟappaṭum.

6. iṉpam aṉpiṉāl nikaḻvatu : kaṟpu
ittuṇaic ciṟantatākiya iṉpa vāḻkkai aṉpiṉāl untappaṭṭē naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉa mutal āciriyar valiyuṟuttiyavāṟu pōlavē, teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvarum,
 aṉpuṟ ṟamarnta vaḻakkeṉpa vaiyakat
tiṉpuṟṟār eytuñ ciṟappu
eṉṟu uṭampāṭṭāṉum,
 aṉpakat tillā uyirvāḻkkai vaṉpāṟkaṇ
vaṟṟaṉ marantaḷirt taṟṟu
eṉṟu etirmaṟaiyāṉum aṉpiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyiṉai viḷaṅka vaittatu kāṇka. maturaik kaṭaiccaṅka kālattukkum muṉṉē paṇṭaic centamiḻk kālattirunta kuṉṟam pūtaṉār eṉṉum nallicaip pulamai malinta cāṉṟōrun tām murukavēḷmēṟ pāṭiya irunilan tuḷaṅkāmai eṉṉum paripāṭalilum,
nāṉmaṟai virittu nallicai viḷakkum
vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ ciṟantatu
kātaṟ kāmaṅ kāmattuc ciṟantatu
eṉṟu kātaliṉpattiṉ māṭci terittu, avviṉpa nukarcci iṟaivaṉatu taṇparaṅkuṉṟattiṉkaṇ nikaḻumāṟṟiṉ vaittu ataṉai virittup pāṭiṉār.
pāruṅkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! ulakiya loḻukkattiṟkum iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭip pēṟṟiṟkum āṇivērpōl iruppatākiya iv iṉpavoḻukkamāṉatu meyyaṉpiṉ vaḻi naṭakkumāyiṉ appōtutāṉ atu kātaliṉpam eṉa uyarttup pēcappaṭumeṉpatum, aḵtu aṅṅaṉam aṉpiṉvaḻi naṭavātāyiṉ iḻinta kāmam eṉa iḻittup pēcappaṭumeṉpatum nam centamiḻt teyva āciriyar ellārkkum otta muṭipā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl ōrāṇmakaṉum oru peṇmakaḷun tammil oruvarai yoruvar uyirpōṟ karuti nirampiya aṉpāl oḻukiṉālaṉṟip, peṇpālārkkuk kaṟpoḻukkam nilaipeṟātu kaṟpoḻukkam nilaipeṟatāyiṉ naṉmakkaḷaip peṟutalum uḷatākātu; ulakil oruvarōṭoruvar amaitiyāyiruntu vāḻāmaṟ pakaimaiyuñ ciṉamuṅ koṇṭa allal uḻappar; ataṉāṟ ṟeyva nampikkaiyun teyva vaṇakkamum illaiyāyp
pōm; eṅkuṅ kuṭiyuṅ kolaiyum koṭumaiyum malintu makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyāṉatu ciṉṉapiṉṉamāyc citaṟiviṭum. ittakaiya kuṭikēṭāṉa ceyal ruṣiyā mutalāṉa mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ paravi aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷai niṉaippiṉuṅ koṭiya tuṉpattiṟ paṭuttut tēyttuvarum nilaiyiṉaic ciṟitāyiṉum aḻuntiyaṟiyavallārkkē, nam centamiḻt teyva āciriyar vaṟpuṟuttic colliya kātaliṉpa vāḻkkaiyiṉ viḻuppam pulaṉākāl niṟkum. maṟṟuk kātalaṉpiṉvayamāki maṇaṅ kūṭiṉārkkō oruvarai viṭṭup piṟaroruvar mēl uḷḷañ cellātākaliṉ, avarkkuk kaṟpoḻukkan tāṉākavē nilaipeṟum; avarkkuk kaṟpoḻukkattiṉaik kaṟpikka vēṟevarumē vēṇṭappaṭār. avar, tām kātal koṇṭa aṉparai yaṉṟip piṟarevaraiyuṅ karutamāṭṭāmaiyāl, tam karuttaṟiyāmal tammaip piṟaroruvarkku maṇam pukunta evarēṉum muṉaikuvarāyiṉ, avarkkut tamatuḷḷak karuttaip pulappaṭuttit tamatu kaṟpoḻukkattiṉai nilainiṟuttik koḷvar; atuvum vāyātāyiṉ tamatuyiraiyē tuṟapparaṉṟit tamatu kaṟpiṟ ciṟitum vaḻuvār; tam kātalar iṟantupaṭiṉ tāmum uyir tāṅkār. ittaṉmaiyarākiya paṇṭait tamiḻ mātariṉ kaṟpoḻukka māṭciyiṉaip, paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ pōnta cila pāṭṭukkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkutum; mutaliṟ kalittokai eṉṉum ariya caṅka nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum oru ceyyuḷai iṅkeṭutturaippām:
cuṭarttoṭīi! kēḷāy! teruvilnām āṭum
maṇaṟciṟṟil kāliṟ citaiyā aṭaicciya
kōtai parintu varippantu koṇṭōṭi
nōtakka ceyyuñ ciṟupaṭṭi mēlōrnāḷ
aṉṉaiyum yāṉum iruntēmā, illirē!
uṇṇunīr vēṭṭēṉ eṉavantāṟ kaṉṉai
aṭarpoṟ cirakattāl vākkic cuṭariḻāy
uṇṇunīr ūṭṭivā veṉṟāḷ, eṉayāṉum
taṉṉai yaṟiyātu ceṉṟēṉ maṟṟeṉṉai
vaḷaimuṉkai paṟṟi naliyat, terumantiṭṭu
aṉṉāy ivaṉoruvaṉ ceytatukāṇ eṉṟēṉā,
aṉṉai yalaṟip paṭartarat, taṉṉaiyāṉ
uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ eṉṟōṉā, aṉṉaiyun
taṉṉaip puṟampuaḻittu nīva, maṟṟeṉṉaik
kaṭaikkaṇṇāṟ kolvāṉpōl nōkki nakaikkūṭṭañ
ceytāṉ akkaḷvaṉ makaṉ.
taṉṉaip piṟaṉoruvaṉukku maṇam poruttak karutit taṉ peṟṟōr ceyyum ēṟpāṭukaḷaik kaṇṭu tikil koṇṭavaḷāṉa aḻaku mikka orukaṟpuṭai maṅkai, tāṉ oruvaṉ mēṟ kātalkoṇṭirukkuṅ kuṟipput taṉ tōḻikkup pulaṉāmpaṭi collutalai icceyyuḷ eṭuttiyampukiṉṟatu; itukēṭṭut taṉ talaiviyiṉ kātaṟ kuṟippaṟinta tōḻi avaṭaṉ peṟṟōrkku ataṉai aṟivittu avaḷ kātalitta iḷaiñaṉukkē avaḷai maṇaṅkūṭṭuvaḷ. itaṉai aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉpar poruḷilakkaṇattil, icceyyuṭ poruḷ varumāṟu :-
viḷaṅkā niṉṟa toṭiyaṇinta tōḻī! eṉmaṉattuḷḷataṉaik kēṭpāyāka! teruvilē nām maṇalāṟ camaittu viḷaiyāṭuñ ciṟiya vīṭṭiṉaik kālāl eṟṟi aḻittum, namatu kūntaliṟ cerukiya pūmālaiyai aṟuttum, varinta namatu pantiṉai eṭuttukkoṇṭōṭiyum nām maṉam nōvattakka kuṟumpukaḷaic ceyyum antac ciṟu paṭṭippaiyaṉ, muṉ oru nāḷ nam aṉṉaiyum yāṉum vīṭṭiṉkaṇ iruntēmāka, empāṟpōntu, vīṭṭil uḷḷīrē! uṇṇappaṭun taṇṇīrkkāka viṭāy koṇṭu vantēṉ eṉṟu collivantu niṉṟaṉukku, aṉṉaiyāṉavaḷ eṉṉai nōkkic cuṭariḻāy! poṟṟakaṭṭāṟ ceyta karakattāl uṇṇunīrai mukantu ceṉṟu vārttu ataṉai avaṉ parukacceytuvā eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. kūṟavē yāṉum avaṉaip paṭṭip paiyaṉ eṉṟu nilaiyāmalē aṉṉai coṉṉapaṭiyē ceyya avaṉṟaṉpāṟ ceṉṟēṉ; āṉāl avaṉ vaḷaiyal aṇinta eṉatu muṉ kaiyiṉaip piṭittiḻuttu vampu ceyyavē, yāṉ maṉam maruṇṭu aṉṉāy! ït‹ xUt‹ brŒjij¥ ghuhŒ! eṉṟu kūviṉōṉāka, uṭaṉē aṉṉai alaṟik koṇṭu eṉpāl varutalum, caṭutiyil yāṉ maṉanteḷintu ivaṉ uṇṇunīraip parukumpōtu vikkivaruntiṉāṉ eṉṟu avaṉ ceytataṉai maṟaittu moḻintēṉ; atu kēṭṭu aṉṉaiyum avaṉ mutukaip palakālun taṭaviṉāḷ; appōtu eṉṉaik kaṭaikkaṇṇāṟ kolvāṉ pōl nōkki, makiḻccikku ētuvāṉa maṉattaik kaḷvaṉākiya avviḷaiñaṉ muṉṉamē ceytaṉaṉ; itaṉai aṟivāyāka!
pārmiṉkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷē! kaṟpiṟ ciṟanta iḷaiya tamiḻ maṅkai orutti taṉṉiḷamaik kālattil nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷil vaittut tāṉ ōr iḷaiñaṉmēṟ kātal koṇṭamaiyiṉai evvaḷavu nuṭpamāka, evvaḷavu tiṟamaiyāka, evvaḷavu maṉakkavarcciyuṇṭākat tāṉkoṇṭa akkātaṟ kaṟpoḻukkattiṟkup paḻutuvārāmaṟ kūṟukiṉṟāḷ! tāṉ ētum aṟiyāp pētaip peṇṇāy irunta kālattilēyē taṉ uḷḷam ōriḷaiñaṉpāl aṉpiṟ patintamaiyum, tāṉ iḻaitta maṇal vīṭṭiṉaik kālāl eṟṟiyun taṉ kūntaliṟ cerukiya mālaiyai aṟuttun tāṉ āṭum pantiṉai eṭuttuk koṇṭōṭiyum avaṉ tāṉ varuntattakka kuṟumpukaḷ ceytum, akkuṟumpukaḷ attaṉaiyun taṉpāl vaitta aṉpiṉālēyē avaṉ ceytamaiyum, ataṉāl akkuṟumpukaḷ taṉmaṉam nōvacceytaṉapōṟ ṟōṉṟiṉum uṇmaiyil avai taṉṉai nōvac ceyyāmaiyum, ciṟupaṭṭi eṉa avaṉaittāṉē cuṭṭuñ collē avaṉpāl taṉakkuḷḷa aṉpiṉ aṟikuṟiyātalum, viṭāy koṇṭu vantāṉeṉiṉum iḷaiñaṉāṉa oruvaṟkut tāṉ taṉiyē ceṉṟu viṭāy taṇittal nāṇuṭaiya peṇṭaṉmaikku ēlāmaiyiṉ aṉṉaiyun tāṉum iruntakkāl avaṉ viṭāyttu vantāṉeṉṟamaiyum, aṅṅaṉam aṉṉaiyōṭu iruntaviṭattum aṉṉaiyiṉ ēvutalālēyē tāṉ nīreṭuttu avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟumaiyum, avaṉ taṉpāl vaitta kātaṟkiḻamaiyāl taṉ kaiyaip piṭittu vampuceyyiṉum ataṟku eḷitilē uṭaṉpaṭṭiruttal accamum nāṇamum iyaṟkaiyē vāynta taṉ peṇṭaṉmaikku ēlāmaiyiṉ ataṟkicaiyātu taṉ aṉṉaiyaik kūviyaḻaittamaiyum, aṉṉai arukuvantatum atupaṟṟi avaṉai avaḷ ciṉavāmaip poruṭṭu avaṉ uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ eṉa avaṉ ceyta kuṟṟattai avaṉmēluḷḷa kātalāl tāṉ maṟaittu moḻintamaiyum, aṉṉaiyum avaṉṟaṉ eḻil nalaṅ kaṇṭu avaṉ vikkutal poṟāyāy avaṉṟaṉ mutukiṉaittaṭavikkoṭuttā ḷeṉṟamaiyum, appōtu tāṉ ceytataṉai oru kuṟumpāka niṉaintu avaṉ taṉṉaik kaṭaikkaṇṇāṟ pārttuc ciṉantamaiyum elm av aṟivuṭaik kaṟparaci ettuṇai nayamākat, taṉ uyarnta peṇṭaṉmaikku ettuṇai icaivāka, iṅṅaṉañ ciṉṉañ ciṟiya paruvam mutalē tāṉkoṇṭa kātaṟ kaṟpoḻukkam ettuṇaic ciṟantateṉpatu pulaṉākak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟāḷ attuṇai viḻumiya kātaṟ kaṟpuṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḻi vanta nam iv varumaiyiṉaik kaṉaviṉum naṉaviṉum niṉaintu niṉaintu viyakkaṟpāla mallamō!
ittuṇaic ciṟanta paṇṭait tamiḻmātariṉ kātaṟ kaṟpoḻukkattai eṭutturaikkum it tamiḻcceyyuḷ vaḻakkum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatā yirukkiṉṟatu. vāṉmati maṇṭila ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉum mēluyarntu celluṅ kūṭa kōpura māḷikaikaḷaik kaṟpittu, nēriṟ ceṉṟu pārttāl oru cila kuṭicaikaḷē yuḷḷa ōr ūril akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ uḷavāka maṉakkōṭṭai kaṭṭi, akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ mēlaṭukku kaḷil uḷḷavarāka mātarkaḷai irutti ammātariṉ muṭimutalaṭikāṟum uḷḷa ovvor uṟuppukaḷaiyum paṟpala ceyyuṭkaḷāl aḻaku paṭuttippāṭi, iṅṅaṉamē iṉṉum illāta poykaḷai yellām ērāḷamāyp puṉaintu kaṭṭic collum ikkālattār ceyyuḷvaḻak keṅkē! uḷḷavāṟē eṭuttiyampic, cintum piḻaipaṭā teḻutiya ōviyam pōlvatākiya paḻantamiḻp pulavariṉ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṅkē! ivviraṇṭiṟkum uḷḷa vēṟu pāṭṭiṉaik kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuḷōṭu ikkalittokaic ceyyuḷaic ciṟitu oppiṭṭu nōkkuvīrkkaḷāyiṉ naṉkaṟivīrkaḷ. atu niṟka.

7. tamiḻ mātariṉ kaṟpu
iṉip, paṇṭaik kātaṟ kaṟpoḻukka mātar taṅ kaṇavar iṟantupaṭṭa ñāṉṟē, avartam piriviṉai āṟṟārāy ēṅki uyirtuṟanta nikaḻccikaḷum pala. ivvāṟu nikaḻntatoru nikaḻcciyiṉaip puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālaiyiṟ pōnta,
 ōruyirāka uṇarka uṭaṉ kalantārk
kīruyi reṉpar iṭaiteriyār - pōril
viṭaṉēntum vēlōṟkum veḷvaḷaiyi ṉāṭkum
uṭaṉē ulanta tuyir
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil ittakaiya nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻntamaiyāl avar avaṟṟiṟku mūtāṉantam eṉap peyariṭṭu, ataṉ ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiṉār, taṉ kātaṟkaṇavaṉ kōvalaṉai iḻanta kaṇṇaki paṭṭa tuyariṉai niṉaippiṟ kaṉṉeñcamuṅ karaiyu maṉṟō? avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉaik kolai ceyvitta pāṇṭiyaṉukku aṟivu teruṭṭip patiṉāṉkām nāḷ taṉṉuyiriṉait tuṟantu taṉ kaṇavaṉuyiroṭu vāṉulakuceṉṟa āṟṟaruṅ kāṭciyiṉaik kuṟavaruṅ kaṇṭaṉa rallarō?
iṉit, taṉkaṇavaṉ pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ māynta ñāṉṟu avaṉaip pirinta tuṉpam poṟāḷāy avaṉṟaṉ kātaṉ maṉaivi peruṅkōppeṇṭu tīppāyac ceṉṟa vaḻi, avvāṟu ceytalākāteṉa vilakkiya cāṉṟōrkku avaḷ,
 palcāṉṟīrē! palcāṉṟīrē!
celkeṉac collā toḻikeṉa vilakkum
pollāc cūḻccip palcāṉṟīrē!
aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy vāḷpōḻn taṭṭa
kāḻpōl nalviḷar naṟuney tīṇṭātu
aṭaiyiṭaik kiṭanta kaipiḻi piṇṭam
veḷḷeṭ cāntoṭu puḷippeytu aṭṭa
vēḷai ventai valci yākap
paraṟpey paḷḷip pāyiṉṟu vatiyum
uyavaṟ peṇṭirēm allēm mātō;
peruṅkāṭṭup paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭ ṭīmam
numakku aritākuka tilla; emakku em
peruntōṭ kaṇavaṉ māynteṉa, arumpaṟa
vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmarai
naḷḷirum poykaiyun tīyum ōraṟṟē
eṉṟu ceyyuḷākavē viṭai kūṟiya nikaḻcci nam tamiḻ naṅkaiyāriṉ kātalaṉ piṉ māṭciyiṉai malaimēl vaitta viḷakkuppōl ulakeṅkum viḷaṅkac ceykiṉṟataṉṟō?
ivvāṟākat tam uyiraiyum oru turumpākak karuti nantam tamiḻp perumāṭṭimār tam kātalar maṭinta vaḻittāmum uṭaṉ maṭiyumāṟu avarai avvāṟu ēviyatu etu avar taṅ koḻunar pāl vaitta oppuyarvaṟṟa kātalaṉ paṉṟō? eṉavē, mātarkkuc ciṟanta kaṟpoḻukkamāvatu avartaṅ kātalaṉpiṉ payaṉāy avarpāl iyaṟkaiyil uḷatāvatē
yaṉṟip, piṟarkku añciyum piṟar kūṟam aṟivurai kēṭṭum varuvataṉṟeṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō iṅṅaṉam iyaṟkai yoḻukkamāy varaṟpālatākiya kaṟpiṟkuk kātalaṉpu oṉṟumē ciṟanta ētuvāyirutlaip pala paṭiyālum makkaḷiyaṟkaiyil vaittu ārāyntu kaṇṭamaiyālaṉṟō āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
 iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku,
aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇai
eṉṟu ōtiyaruḷiṉār, ammutalāciriyar vaḻivanta tiruvaḷḷuvar mutalāṉa cāṉṟōrum aṅṅaṉamē aḵtu aṉpiṉ vaḻi naṭakkaṟpāṟṟeṉṟu paṇṭu toṭṭu iṉṟu kāṟum valiyuṟutti vantirukkiṉṟaṉa rallarō?

8. iññāṉṟai maṉai vāḻkkai
āṉāl, ippōtu naṭaipeṟum maṉaivāḻkkai ettaṉmaiyāy irukkiṉtu? aḵtu ulakiya loḻukkattiṟku uyirpōlvatākiya kātalaṉpiṉ vaḻiccolkiṉṟatā? illai, illai, ciṟitumēyillai. cātivēṟṟumai yeṉṉum koṭiyatūkkukkayiṟāṉatu kātalaṉpiṉ kaḻuttai iṟukkiviṭṭatu; kātalaṉpiṟ ciṟantu maṟuvaṟṟamatipōl viḷaṅkat takkavarāṉa nam peṇmaṇikaḷiṉ kaṟpoḻukkattai nilaikulaittu ataṉaip paḻipāvaṅkaḷāl mūṭiviṭṭatu? entap peṇmakaḷāvatu tāṉ kātalitta iḷaiñaṉai maṇaṅkūṭa iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉaḷā? eḷḷaḷavum illaiyē, ēṉ? oru cātikkuḷḷē tāṉ, orupatu vīṭukaḷē yuḷḷa ōr iṉattilē tāṉ avaḷ oruvaṉai maṇakkavēṇṭum. avaḷ kayalmīṉaiyotta kaṇṇaḻakiyā yiruntāleṉṉa! kaṇkuruṭāṉa oruvaṉait tavira vēṟu maṇamakaṉ taṉ iṉattiṟ kiṭaittilaṉāyiṉ avaḷ avaḷaiyētāṉ maṇantu tīral vēṇṭum! avaḷ muttuk kōttālotta pallaḻakiyāy iruntāleṉṉa! taṉ pāḻum iṉattil oru pokkai vāykkiḻavaṉait tavira vēṟumaṇamakaṉ kiṭaittilaṉāyiṉ, avaḷ avaṉaiyētāṉ maṇantutīral vēṇṭum! avaḷ palakalai kaṟṟuk kalviyaṟivilum icai pāṭuvatilum nuṇṇaṟivilum civanēya aṭiyār nēyaṅkaḷiluñ ciṟanta kaṭṭaḻakiyā yiruntāleṉṉa! iṟumāppē kuṭikoṇṭa taṉciṟumākkuṭiyiṟ kalviyirunta mūlaiyē kaṇṭaṟiyātavaṉum, pāṭṭuppāṭa vāyait tiṟantāl ōṭṭamāy vara vaṇṇāṉai varuvippāṉum, aṟivai ōṭṭiviṭṭa veṟumūḷai yuṭaiyāṉuṅ, kaṟuppaṇṇaṉ maturaivīraṉ māriyammaṉ mutalāṉa veṟukkattakka pēykaṭku ūṉuṅkaḷḷumpaṭaittuk kuṭittu veṟippāṉumākiya oruvaṉait tavira vēṟu maṇamakaṉ kiṭaittilaṉāyiṉ, avaṉ taṉ cātiyaiviṭṭu vēṟu cātiyiṟ kalakkalākāmaiyiṉ, akkallāk kayavaṉaiyē kaṇavaṉāyk koḷḷal vēṇṭum! ā! poruḷaṟṟa ic cātivēṟṟumaik koṭumaiyāl nam arumaip peṇmaṇikaḷ paṭuntuyar malaiyiṉum peritō! aṉṟik kaṭaliṉum peritō! aṉṟi ñālattiṉum peritō! ivvaḷavutāṉ eṉṟu kūṟal em oru nāvāl iyalātu!
pala nalaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟanta or āṇmakaṉum aṅṅaṉamē taṉ cātiyai viṭṭup piṟa cātiyiṟ kalattal ākāmaiyāl nalañ ciṟitumillā oru pōtaip peṇṇai maṇantu tuṉpuṟa vēṇṭiyavaṉā yiruntālum, avaṉatu nilai oruciṟanta peṇṇiṉ tuṉpa nilaipōl attuṇaik koṭitaṉṟu.
ēṉeṉṟāl, avaṉ tāṉ vēṇṭiyapaṭi vēṟu makaḷiraik kūṭikkaḷittālum, avaṉai mikutiyāyk kuṟṟañ colvār illai. āṉāl, nam arumaip peṇmakkaḷiṉ nilaiyō attakaiya taṉṟē! avarkaḷ yāraippārttāluṅ kuṟṟam, yāroṭu pēciṉālum ataṉiluṅ kuṟṟam, appuṟañcolla vēṇṭuvatu yātuḷatu? vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷuṅ kūṭat tān tām virumpiya tuṇaiyaiyē kūṭi iṉitu vāḻāniṟka, āṟaṟivuṭaiya makkaṭ piṟaviyiṟ piṟantu aṟivaiyum aḻakaiyum naṟkuṇattaiyuṅ kaṇṭu pārāṭṭik kātalaṉpu koḷḷattakka āṇ peṇ makkaḷ maṭṭum iṭaiyē pukutta cātivēṟṟumai yeṉṉuṅ kolaiñaṉāl veṭṭiyaḻikkappaṭṭuk kātalaṉpu eṉṉum uyir ōṭippōṉa piṉ, iḻinta kāmappiṇa vāḻkkaiyiṟ kālaṅkaḻikkumpaṭi kaṭṭāyap paṭuttappaṭṭāl, itaṉiṉum vēṟu koṭuṅ kolai uḷatō! kūṟumiṉkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷē! ulakiya loḻukkattiṟku aṭippaṭaiyāy niṟkuṅ kātalaṉpaik kīḻaṟuttuviṭṭāl, ataṉ mēl niṟkum avvulakiyal vāḻkkai kīḻ viḻuntu nuṟuṅkā toḻiyumō! iṅṅaṉam makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyai vērilēyē aṟuttuk kolaipurivōr, aruḷoḻukkattaiyum aṉpoḻukkattaiyumē valiyuṟuttum nam teyva āciriyarāl vakukkappaṭṭa nam caiva camayattiṟku uriyarāvarō! coṉmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē!

9. kāraikkālammaiyār maṉaiyaṟam
nam arumaic caivacamayamāṉatu kātalaṉpiṉ vaḻi naṭakkun tūya vāḻkkaiyiṉaiyē vēṇṭuvataṉṟi, ataṉaik kolai ceyyuñ cātivēṟṟumai vāḻkkaiyiṉaic ciṟitum vēṇṭuvataṉ ṟeṉpataṟku nam caiva nāyaṉmār varalāṟukaḷē cāṉṟātal ciṟitu kāṭṭutum. kāraikkāl ammaiyār eṉṉuñ caivap peṇmaṇiyāriṉ varalāṟṟai muṉṉar ārāyvām. ivar teyvattaṉmai vāynta naṅkaiyārātaluṭaṉ, tamiḻmoḻik kaṭalaiyum ārapparuki ataṉkaṇ ariyaperiya ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷuḷ iyaṟṟun tiṟam vāyntavar. aḻakilō kaival kammiyaṉ tiruttic camaitta arumpeṟaṟpoṟpāvaiyai oppavar. ciṉṉañ ciṟu paruvam mutalē civapirāṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇṇuñ civaṉaṭiyār pālum mikka aṉpu pūṇṭavar. ivvaḷavu ciṟanta oru teyvamaṅkaikku ēṟṟa kaṇavaṉai avar tāmē terintu kātalittal vēṇṭumaṉṟip, piṟar avarkkēṟṟa oruvaṉait teriya māṭṭuvārallar. aṅṅaṉa miruntum avartañ cuṟṟattār tam vaṇikamarapiṉāṉa oruvaṉaik koṇarntu avarkkuk kaṇavaṉāka maṇam porutti viṭṭaṉar. avvāṟu maṇañ ceyvikkappaṭṭa avaṉ ammaiyār tam arumaikaḷai muṉṉarē yaṟintu avaṟṟiṟku ēṟpak kātalaṉpu pūṇṭu naṭakkat takka māṭci vāyntavaṉ allaleṉpatu, avaratu teyvat taṉmaiyai aṟinta vaḷavil avaṉ avarait toṭutaṟku añciyiruntu, piṉṉarc cilanāṭkaḷil avar aṟiyāmē pirintu vēṟoru nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkē vēṟoruttiyai maṇantu koṇṭiruntamaiyāṉum, atu terinta ammaiyāriṉ uṟaviṉar avarai oru civikaiyil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu avaṉirunta ūrkkuppōṉa kālaiyil avaṉ taṉ iraṇṭām maṉaivi makkaḷuṭaṉ etirpōntu ammaiyārai maṉaiviyāka nilaiyāmal teyvamāka niṉaintu avartan tiruvaṭikaḷil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉamaiyāṉum naṉku peṟappaṭum. ammaiyārum avaṉpāṟ kātalaṉpu nikaḻappeṟāmaṟ kaṇavaṉeṉṟakaṭamaiyai niṉaintu oruvāṟu aṉpupūṇṭoḻukiṉa reṉpataṟku. avaṉ taṉṉai vaṇaṅki niṉṟa kālaiyil avaṉ poruṭṭut tāṅkiya aḻakiya uṭampiṉ vaṭivait tacaip poti eṉa veṟuttuk kūṟi ataṉai māṟṟip pēy vaṭivu taṉakkut tarumāṟu avar iṟaivaṉai vēṇṭiṉamaiyē cāṉṟām; iḵtu āciriyar cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷ,
īṅkivaṉ kuṟitta koḷkai ituiṉi ivaṉuk kākat
tāṅkiya vaṉappu niṉṟa tacaip poti kaḻittiṅkuṉpāl
āṅkunariṉ tāḷkaḷ pōṟṟum pēyvaṭi vaṭiyē ṉukkup
pāṅkuṟa vēṇṭu meṉṟu paramaṉaip paravi niṉṟār
eṉak kūṟamāṟṟāl teḷippaṭum. kātalaṉpu eṉpatu ōrāviṟku irukōṭu tōṟṟiṉāṟpōla iruvaruyirum ōruyirāy īruṭampiṉ niṉṟu iyaiyum perukiya tūya aṉpākum. iḵtu āciriyar nakkīraṉār iṟaiyaṉārākap poruḷuraiyiṟ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāril,
kākat tirukaṇṇiṟ koṉṟē maṇikalan tāṅkiruvar
ākattuḷ ōruyir kaṇṭaṉam yāmiṉṟi yāvaiyumām
mēkat toruvaṉ irumpoḻi lampala vaṉmalaiyil
tōkaikkun tōṉṟaṟkum oṉṟāy varumiṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷē
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytamaiyāṉum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. ippeṟṟit
tākiya kātalaṉpu kāraikkālammaiyārkkum avar taṅ kaṇavaṉārkkum nikaḻappeṟāmaiyāl, avartañ cērkkai veṟṟuṭampiṉ cērkkaiyā yiruntē yallāmal, aṉpāl uruki oṉṟupaṭṭa uyiriṉ cērkkaiyāy iruntatiṉṟu. ammaiyāraip pōlavē avaṉuṅ kalaittamiḻp pulamaiyil nirampic civanēya aṭiyār nēyaṅkaḷilum mikkiruntaṉa ṉāyiṉ, avaṉ avarkkēṟṟa kaṇavaṉāka, avartañ cērkkai uyiriṉ cērkkaiyēyā yiruntirukkum. avvāṟākāmaikkuk kāraṇam ammaiyārē tamakkut takkāṉoruvaṉait teriyātirukka, avartañ cuṟṟattār avviruvariyalpum āyntāyntu pārāmal avar tammaip piṇaittu viṭṭamaiyē yākum.

10. cuntarartam kātalaṉpiṉ vāḻkkai
iṉik, kātalaṉpiṟ ciṟanta iṉpavāḻkkai iṭainiṉṟa cuṟṟattāriṉ muyaṟciyāṟ citaivupaṭṭuk kāraikkālammaiyārkku iṉitu vāyātu pōṉamai pōlavē, cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉārkkuṅ kātal maṉaivāḻkkai naṉku vāyātu pōyirukkum; āṉāl, ellām palla iṟaivaṉ avarpāl mikka irakkampūṇṭu avarkku aṅṅaṉam nēra voṭṭāmal taṭaiceytaruḷiṉaṉ. pāruṅkaḷ! cuntara mūrttikaḷiṉ cuṟṟattārum, avartam pēraḻaku aruntamiḻp pulamai civanēyam mutaliyavaikaḷai āḻntāyntu pārttu, avartam teyva māṭcikku otta oru maṇamakaḷait terin
teṭuttavarallar; tamatu caiva annataṇamarapil tāmvēṇṭiya oru peṇṇait terintu koṇṭu avarkku avaḷai maṇamporuttat tuvaṅkiṉar. atu kaṇṭa pēraruṭ kaṭalāṉa civapirāṉ ōr antaṇa mutiyōṉāy vantu, accuṟṟattār kūṭṭiya maṇam naṭaipeṟāvāṟu taṭaiceytu, avarait tiruveṇṇey nallūrkku īrttup pōy āṭkoṇ ṭaruḷiṉar. ataṉpiṟ cuntara mūrttikaḷ iṟaivaṉpāl aṉpu mītūrappeṟṟup, pala tirukkōyilkaḷai vaṇaṅkikkoṇṭu, tiruvārūrkku vanta kālaiyiṟ civapirāṉ tirukkōyilil āṭal pāṭalkaḷākiya tiruttoṇṭu ceytu koṇṭirunta iḷanaṟuṅ kaṉṉit teyva naṅkaiyārāṉa paravainācciyāraik kaṇṭu uḷḷamum uyirum uruki yoruṅkē avarpāṟ patiyap peṟṟārāyk kaḻiperuṅ kātalaṉpu koṇṭu,
 kaṟpakattiṉ pūṅkompō kāmaṉṟaṉ peruvāḻvō
poṟpuṭaiya puṇṇiyattiṉ puṇṇiyamō puyalcumantu
viṟkuvaḷai pavaḷamalar matipūtta viraikkoṭiyō
aṟputamō civaṉaruḷō aṟiyēṉeṉ ṟaticayittār
aṅṅaṉamē avaraik kaṇṭa paravai nācciyārum itaṟku muṉ tām kaṇṭaṟiyāta oru teyvakkātal koḷḷappeṟṟu,
 muṉṉēvan tetirtōṉṟum murukaṉō perukoḷiyāṟ
ṟaṉṉēril māraṉō tārmārpiṉ viñcaiyaṉō
miṉṉērceñ caṭaiyaṇṇal meyyaruḷpeṟ ṟuṭaiyavaṉō
eṉṉēeṉ maṉantirinta ivaṉyārō eṉaniṉaintār
pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! itteyvap perumāṉum perumāṭṭiyum oruvarai yoruvar kātalittu, akkātalaṉpiṉ vayamāy uṇarvu celluṅkāluñ civaperumāṉ ṟiruvaruḷē ivvāṟu uruvukoṇṭu tōṉṟiyatu pōlum eṉak karuti viyakkiṉṟār! teyvattai maṟantu, uyarnta nōkkaṅkaḷai maṟantu iḻinta ūṉuṭampiṉ cērkkaiyilēyē niṉaivu ceṉṟu kāmaṅ kāḻppēṟi niṟkum ulakattu makkaḷiṉ kāma uṇarccikkum, civapirāṉ ṟiruvaruḷilēyē niṉaivaḻunti avvaruḷ viḷakkamākavē tam kātalarai niṉaikkuñ cuntarar paravaiyāriṉ teyvak kātal uṇarccikkum evvaḷavu vēṟṟumai! tammuḷ otta iyalpiṉarāṉa teyvak kātalar maṇavāḻkkaikkuc cuntara mūrttikaḷiṉ maṇa vāḻkkai yiṉuñ ciṟantatoṉṟaṉai ivvulakil vēṟeṅkaṇuṅ kāṇṭalaritu. iṅṅaṉamē, ivar tam maṟṟai maṉaiviyarāṉa caṅkili nācciyārkkum ivarkkum uṇṭāṉa toṭarpun teyvak kātaliṉpāṟ paṭṭatākum.
aṟṟēl, oruvar iruvaraik kātalittal iyalumō veṉiṉ; āṇmakaṟ kāyiṉ aḵtiyalum; eṉṉai?
perumaiyum uraṉum āṭūumēṉa
eṉṟum,
 accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum muntuṟatta
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriyaeṉpa
eṉṟum āciriyar tolkāppiyār āṇpeṇ iyaṟkai vevvēṟātalai aṟivuṟuttalāṉum, ivviyaṟkai vēṟupāṭṭiṉai naṉkuṇarntē nākarika makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyiṉuḷ ōrāṇmakaṉ maṉaiviyar palarai maṇakkap palarum icaital pōl oru peṇ makaṉ āṇmakkaḷ palarai maṇakka erum icaivatillāmaiyāṉum, cuntaramūrttikaḷ iruvaraik kātalittal āṇpā liyaṟkaikku ottatēyām ākalāṉum eṉpatu.
mēluñ, cuntara mūrttikaḷiṉ teyvat taṉmaikku otta peṇpālār iruvar iruppa, appeṇpālār iruvariṉ teyvak kātaṟṟaṉmaikku otta āṇpālār iruvar ilarāyc cuntaramūrttikaḷ oruvarē uḷarāyiṉ, avar kātalālum aruḷāluma untappaṭṭu iruvarai maṇattal vaḻakkēyām. itupōṉṟa ariya kātal nikaḻcciyiṉai nakkīrar māṇikkavācakar mutalāṉa tollāciriyarum uṭaṉpaṭṭut taḻuvutal niṉaiviṟ patikkappāṟṟu. ivvāṟu iruvaruyirum uruki oṉṟupaṭṭu niṟkum pēriṉ nilaiyō ṭoppatākaliṉ, aṉpuruvām iṟaivaṉum avartam teyvak kātaṟ peruṅkiḻamaikku ottu utaviyāy niṉṟaruḷiṉaṉ. itaṉālaṉṟō cuntaramūrttikaḷ,
paṉmayatta moḻipparavai caṅkilikkum eṉakkum
paṟṟāya perumāṉē maṟṟārai yuṭaiyēṉ
eṉṟaruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar.

11. cātivēṟṟumai aṉpukku māṟu
kātalaṉpu nikaḻuṅ kālattu atu nikaḻap peṟuvār iruvar cāti kula vēṟṟumai ciṟitum pārātu avvaṉpiṉ vaḻiyarāyc ceṉṟu oruṅku cērvareṉpatum, cāti kula vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyē pārttuk koṇṭu iṟumāntirukkum potumakkaḷ tūya kātalaṉpu nikaḻap peṟutaṟkut takkāraik kaṇṭukoḷḷa māṭṭuvāralla reṉpatum, aruḷirakkam vāynta iṟaivaṉē orōvorukāṟ kātalaṉpuṭaiyārai oṉṟu cērttaṟ poruṭṭup potumakkaḷ ākkuñ cātivēṟṟumaik kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷai muṟṟac citaippaṉeṉpatum, cuntaramūrttikaḷ eṉṉuñ caiva antaṇar perumāṉ uruttikaṇikaiyar kulattiṟ piṟanta paravai nācciyāraiyum vēḷāḷar kulattiṟ piṟanta caṅkili nācciyāraiyun tamatu kātal ceṉṟa vaḻiyē maṇantu koṇṭamaiyāṉum, ataṟkumuṉ avartañ cuṟṟattār avaratu teyvamāṭci aṟiyātē avariyalpukkut takāta oruttiyait tamatu marapiṉiṉṟum eṭuttu maṇam poruttap pukkālaiyil ataṉai iṟaivaṉē nēr niṉṟu taṭaiceytu, piṉṉar avariyalpukku muḻutumotta paravai caṅkiliyārai maṇam poruntiṉamaiyāṉun teḷiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa vallavō? kātalaṉpu koḷḷattakkār iruvaraip potumakkaḷ aṟiya māṭṭamaiyum, iṟaivaṉē avarai yaṟintu oṉṟu cērttalum māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceyta,
 coṟpāl amiḻtivaḷ yāṉ cuvai yeṉṉat tuṇintiṅṅaṉē
naṟpāl niṉaitteyvan tantiṉṟu nāṉiva ḷāmpakutip
poṟpuā raṟivār puliyūrp puṉitaṉ potiyilveṟpiṟ
kaṟpā viyavarai vāykkaṭitōṭṭa kaḷavakattē
eṉṉum arumait tirukkōvaiyār ceyyuḷāṉum iṉitu pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa vallavō?
ākavē, caivacamayattiṟ piṟanta ovvoruvarun, tam camayāciriya rākiya cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉār nilainiṟuttik kāṭṭiya kātaliṉpa vāḻkkai ulakil niṉṟu nilavumāṟu tam āṇ peṇ makkaḷai attakaiya uṇmaiyaṉpiṉ maṉaivāḻkkaiyil amaracceytu, namatu paḻantamiḻk koḷkaiyākiya caiva camaya nalattiṉai eṅkum parappi, nam makkaḷaip paḻipāvaṅkaḷukku āḷākkātu iḷamai toṭṭē avarkaḷatu uḷḷam uṇmaiyaṉpil ūṟi oḷirum vaṇṇañ ceytal mikavum iṉṟiyamai yātatākum.
iṉip, paḻantamiḻk kōṭpāṭākiya caivacamaya uṇmai ivvāṟiruppavum, itaṉai aṟiyamāṭṭārāy, āriyak kurukkaḷmār tamatu nalattiṉ poruṭṭup puṉaintu kaṭṭikoṇṭa cāti vēṟṟumaiyiṉaik kuruṭṭup piṭiyāyp piṭittu, avvavarun tattam cātivarampu kaṭavātu tattam iṉattilēyē uṇṇal kalattalkaḷaic ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, apparum appūtikaḷum cātivēṟṟumai pārātu oruṅkiruntu aḷavaḷāvi uṇavu koṇṭatum, cuntaramūrttikaḷum aṅṅaṉamē cātivēṟṟumai pārātu kātalaṉpiṉ vaḻicceṉṟu paravai caṅkiliyāra maṇantu koṇṭatum apperiyārkku okkum. maṟṟu, avarpōl uyarnta pēraṉpu nilaiyiṉiṉṟu aṉpillā iḻinta nilakkaṇ niṟkum nammaṉōr cātikula vēṟṟumai kaṭantu avvāṟu uṇṇal kalattalkaḷaic ceytalākāteṉṟun tammaik koḻutta caivareṉak kūṟip perumai pārāṭṭik koḷvār cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivartaṅ kūṟṟu nammaṉōrai muṉṉēṟṟam aṭaiya veṭṭātu paḻipāvaṅkaṭ paṭukkun tīmaiyuṭait tātalaiyum, paṇṭai nallāciriyar koḷkaikaḷellāñ cātivarampaip citaittoḻikkum peṟṟiya vātalaiyuñ cāti vēṟṟumaiyum pōlic caivarum eṉṉum ematu nūlil virittuk kāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟēm. iṅkēyum ivar kūṟṟup piḻai pāṭātalaic ciṟitu kāṭṭukiṉṟēm. uyarntōrākiya nam āciriyar kāṭṭiya ōr oḻukkam ciṟanta toṉṟāyiṉ, atu piḻaipāṭilatāyiṉ avarvaḻi niṟkum nām avar kāṭṭiya ammuṟaiyē oḻukak kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm; avvāṟaṉṟi avarkāṭṭiya avvoḻukkañ ciṟanta tallātāyp piḻaipaṭuva toṉṟāyiṉ nām ataṉaip piṉpaṟṟi naṭattal ākātu. apparum appūtikaḷuñ cātikula vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyē nōkkikkoṇṭiruntaṉarāyiṉ avarkkuḷḷa oppuyarvillāp pēraṉpu paḻutupaṭum; ātalāl, uyarntatākiya aṉpainōkki iḻintatākiya cātivēṟṟumaiyiṉai avar appāṟ paṭuttiṉar aṅṅaṉamē, cuntaramūrttikaḷuñ cāti vēṟṟumaiyiṉaip peritāka niṉaintiruntaṉarāyiṉ, tammōṭotta civanēyac ciṟappum pēraṟivum pēraḻakum vāynta paravaiyār caṅkiliyār eṉṉum nallāriṇakkattai iḻanta viṭṭavarākal vēṇṭum; maṟṟuppuṉitamākiya avaruḷḷat teḻunta peruṅ kātalaṉpō accāti vēṟṟumaiyiṉai nuṟuṅkat tolaittup peṟaṟkariya annallāriṇakkattaiyē nāṭic ceṉṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, muṟṟat tuṟanta paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,
nallā riṇakkamum niṉpūcai nēcamum ñāṉamumē
allātu vēṟu nilaiyuḷatō
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉa rallarō? oruvarkku uṭalum, uyirumāy irukkattakkār avarāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭavarē yallāṟ piṟarallarē. ēṉaiyōr iṇakkamellāñ ciṟcilakālamē nilaippatu. tāytantaiya riṇakkam, putalvariṇakkam, āciriyariṇakkam, tōḻariṇakkam, uṭaṉpiṟantāriṇakkam mutaliyaṉa vellāñ ciṟcila kālamēnikaḻvataṉṟi, muḻutun toṭarntu nikaḻā. maṟṟuk, kātalaṉpiṟ ciṟanta kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum iṇaṅkum iṇakkamō avartam vāḻ nāḷellaiyaḷavum iravum pakalum piriyātāyt toṭarntu nikaḻun taṉmaiyatām. atilum avar aḻakilum aṟivilum aṉpiluñ civanēyattilum mikkavarāyiruntāl avartam iṇakkattiṉ māṭciyiṉaiyum iṉpattiṉaiyum eṅṅaṉam uraikkēm! evvāṟu pukaḻvēm! pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! cuntaramūrttikaṭkum avartam maṉaiviyarkkum vāytta nēyamāṭci emmāl aḷapparitākaliṉ avar cātivēṟṟumai tolaittuk kaikkoṇṭa annēyam emmaṉōrk vaḻikāṭṭum maṅkāp peruviḷakkamāy irukkaṟpāla taṉṟō!

12. cāti vēṟṟumai evarkkum ākātu
periyōrāṉa avarkkāyiṉ cātivēṟṟumai kaṭattaltakum, ciṟiyōrāṉa emakkō atu takātu eṉpirēṟ, ciṟiyōrāṉa yām periyōrātal eppaṭi? periyōr kāṭṭiya naṉ muṟaiyiṉaip piṉpaṟṟi yoḻukiṉālaṉṟō yāmum periyōrākalām. avar kāṭṭiya naṉmuṟaiyiṉaip piṉpaṟṟātu ennēramuñ cāticcaṇṭaiyilēyē cerukkuṟṟu māḻkuvamāyiṉ yām periyōrātal evvāṟu kaikūṭum? avar kāṭṭiya pēraṉpu muṟaiyiṉaikkaṭaip piṭiyātu ennēramum aṉpukku māṟāṉa vampilēyē nāṅkālaṅkaḻittāl nām periyōrātal yāṅṅaṉam kaikūṭum? periyōr ceytatoṉṟu takātateṉa namakkut tōṉṟu māyiṉ, ataṉai nām ceyyātu viṭalām. apparum appūtiyum pēraṉpāl aḷavaḷāvic cātivēṟṟumaiyiṉaip pārātu oḻukiyamuṟai uyarntatā, tāḻntatatā? tāḻnta teṉṟu kūṟa evarumē oruppaṭārātalāl, uyarnta annaṉmuṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi naṭattalāl namakkuvarum iḻukkeṉṉai? ellā makkaḷum oru tantaiyiṉ piḷḷaikaḷē yātalāl avarellārum uṭaṉpiṟappurimai pārāṭṭi ellāvaṟṟālum oruṅku aḷavaḷāvutalē naṉṟeṉṟu ēcunātarum makamatu napiyuṅ kūṟiya aṟivuraikaḷaik kēṭṭu mēlnāṭṭu makkaḷellārum ētoru  vēṟu pāṭumiṉṟi oṉṟupaṭṭu oḻuki ulakiṟ cīruñ ciṟappum eyti varutalaik kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭuvaittum, nam teyva āciriyarkaḷ oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum eṉṟu muṭittukkūṟiya aṟavuraiyiṉai nām kaṭaippiṭiyātu vāḻnāḷ ellaiyaḷavuñ cātiyiṟumāppup pēccaiyē pēcik, kūṟṟuvaṉukku iraiyāy oḻital piṟanāṭṭavarāṟ peritum iḻitturaikkap paṭukiṉṟataṉṟō? cāti vēṟṟumaiyāl nām peṟṟa nalam eṉṉai? oru ciṟu nalamāyiṉum iruntāl ataṉai nām kaikkoḷḷalām. āṉāl, oruvaṉ taṉṉaiyuyarnta cātiyāṉākac cerukkuṭaṉ uyarttuppēcip piṟaṉoruvaṉai iḻinta cātiyāṉāka iḻitturaikkuṅkāl, aṅṅaṉam iḻikkappaṭṭavaṉ evvaḷavu maṉam naikiṉṟāṉ! avaṉ āṇmai yuṭaiyavaṉā yiruntāl taṉṉai iḻittup pēciṉavaṉait tāṉum iḻittup pēca ataṉāl iruvarkkuḷḷuṅ kalām viḷaikiṉṟataṉṟō? ivvāṟu aṉpiṉaic citaittu oṉṟumaiyiṉaip pāḻpaṭuttuñ cātivēṟṟumai yiṉai nam āciriyaṉmā rellārum oruṅkē  yutaittuttaḷḷi aṉpiṉ vaḻiyarāy naṭantukāṭṭiyirukkaiyil, nām avaraip piṉpaṟṟātu pōvōmāyiṉ, atu nam āciriyarkkuc ceyta perum piḻaiyāy nammaic caivacamayattiṟkup puṟampākkumeṉṟu tiṇṇamāy uṇarmiṉkaḷ!
